Normalize everything to strings (HWPX text nodes are strings)\n    mapping: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        mapping[str(k)] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef paragraph_text_nodes(p_el: ET.Element) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    # Collect hp:t elements in document order under this paragraph.\n    return list(p_el.findall(\".//hp:t\", NS))\n\n\ndef join_text(nodes: list[ET.Element]) -> str:\n    parts: list[str] = []\n    for n in nodes:\n        parts.append(n.text or \"\")\n    return \"\".join(parts)\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:\n    used: set[str] = set()\n\n    def _sub(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in mapping:\n            used.add(key)\n            return mapping[key]\n        # Unknown placeholder: leave as-is.\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(_sub, text), used\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_children(p_el: ET.Element) -> None:\n    # Only remove the layout-cache elements within this paragraph.\n    # Observed in template: <hp:linesegarray> ...\n    # Also handle common variants in other documents.\n    cache_names = {\n        \"linesegarray\",\n        \"lineSegArray\",\n        \"linesegArray\",\n        \"lineSeg\",\n        \"lineseg\",\n    }\n\n    # Only direct children should be removed (per instruction: \"child elements\"),\n    # but be robust and remove any descendants matching cache names.\n    for parent in list(p_el.iter()):\n        for child in list(parent):\n            if localname(child.tag) in cache_names:\n                parent.remove(child)\n\n\ndef process_xml(xml_bytes: bytes, mapping: dict[str, str], zip_member: str) -> tuple[bytes, dict]:\n    raw_text = xml_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\")\n\n    placeholders_in_file = sorted(set(PLACEHOLDER_RE.findall(raw_text)))\n    missing = sorted([k for k in placeholders_in_file if k not in mapping])\n\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes)\n\n    changed_paragraphs = 0\n    used_keys: set[str] = set()\n\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        t_nodes = paragraph_text_nodes(p)\n        if not t_nodes:\n            continue\n\n        original = join_text(t_nodes)\n        # Preserve truly empty paragraphs: nothing to replace.\n        if original == \"\":\n            continue\n\n        replaced, used = replace_placeholders(original, mapping)\n        if replaced == original:\n            continue\n\n        used_keys |= used\n\n        # Read template zip and process in-memory.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        members = zin.namelist()\n        xml_targets: list[str] = []\n        for m in members:\n            # Only consider files; directory entries end with '/'\n            if m.endswith(\"/\"):\n                continue\n            try:\n                data = zin.read(m)\n            except KeyError:\n                continue\n            if b\"{{\" in data:\n                xml_targets.append(m)\n\n        reports = []\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"w\", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            for m in members:\n                if m.endswith(\"/\"):\n                    # Preserve directory entries if present\n                    zout.writestr(m, b\"\")\n                    continue\n\n                data = zin.read(m)\n                if m in xml_targets and m.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                    new_bytes, info = process_xml(data, mapping, m)\n                    reports.append(info)\n                    zout.writestr(m, new_bytes)\n                else:\n                    zout.writestr(m, data)\n\n    # Basic mapping coverage report to stdout (for the harness logs).\n    all_placeholders = sorted({p for r in reports for p in r[\"placeholders_in_file\"]})\n    missing_total = sorted({p for r in reports for p in r[\"missing_placeholders\"]})\n\n    print(\"Processed members containing placeholders:\")\n    for r in reports:\n        print(\n            f\"- {r['zip_member']}: changed_paragraphs={r['changed_paragraphs']}, \"\n            f\"placeholders={len(r['placeholders_in_file'])}, missing={len(r['missing_placeholders'])}\"\n        )\n\n    if all_placeholders:\n        print(\"\\nPlaceholders seen in template:\")\n        for p in all_placeholders:\n            print(f\"  - {p}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nNo placeholders found in the template.\")\n\n    if missing_total:\n        print(\"\\nWARNING: placeholders missing from JSON mapping (left unchanged):\")\n        for p in missing_total:\n            print(f\"  - {p}\")\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"07bf109d-a46b-4a9c-81ca-90e0eb44485d","uuid":"b245f1bd-66f6-4c89-9ffe-12addb3ac192","timestamp":"2026-07-16T03:19:15.103Z"}
